## 0.0.32
- make lint-php runs phpstan.dist.neon on packages/server/php again: kit 0.0.98 reads the phpstan.neon of a package folder first, so the level max configuration of make analyse-php is named phpstan-max.neon (H27.85).
- Release 0.0.32 after H27.81, H27.82 and H27.83: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.32 (H27.84).
- hyper supports kit 0.0.98, which includes no recipe, sets no setting of another tool and writes no copy into a repository: the Makefile includes no file of kit and holds every target that its recipes and workflows run, each running a utility of the installed kit with explicit arguments; mise.toml states the settings of mise and uv, .npmrc and tests/package-install/.npmrc state allow-remote, and knip.json, .oxlintrc.json, ruff.toml, phpcs.xml.dist and phpstan.dist.neon state the lint rules; the entry gates of ci.yml runs the checkers of kit in place of the copy ci-pipeline.yml; release.yml and the bootstrap action are files of hyper; the copies .githooks, .claude/settings.json, config/ci.json, config/cargo.schema.json and config/pyproject.schema.json are removed; PRINCIPLES is kit's copy whose Enforced by lines name the checks that hyper runs, the entry gates also runs make rules-check, and make check and the tests rely on no FINAL_RUN, ci-only refusal or Claude Code hook (H27.83).
- Both Composer roots require the json-schema fork by its release 2.6.0-p2 in place of its branch polyspec-origin-2.6.0, and hyper adopts kit 0.0.95, whose PRINCIPLES leave the rule of forks to the FORK.md of each fork and the manifest and lock of each root (H27.82).
- docs/operations/deployment.md states that a persistent connection of phpredis verifies the TLS certificate only when it opens, so a changed CA applies after php-fpm reloads, and that a running worker does not detect a wrong CA (H27.81).

## 0.0.31
- Release 0.0.31 after H27.79: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.31 (H27.80).
- A PHP request that cannot reach the Redis server no longer replaces the session cookie of the client: with a lost connection the session handler of phpredis fails to validate the identifier, so PHP queued the cookie of a new identifier before the read failed, and the answer of status 500 carried it, which ended the session and refused the next form with status 403. A session that cannot start now withdraws that cookie, and tests read the cookie of each answer of status 500 on the three servers (H27.79).

## 0.0.30
- Release 0.0.30 after H26.9 and H27.77: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.30 (H27.78).
- A Python server on Redis Sentinel no longer splits a session after a promotion: the `setup` option of RedisSessions asks Sentinel before the first command of a request that has a session, where a pooled connection of redis-py stayed on the former primary, which accepts writes until it becomes a replica, and answered status 403 for the sessions of the new primary. A test promotes the replica while the primary runs and shares new sessions across the three servers in that window (H27.77).
- docs/operations/benchmark.md holds the record of two runs of `.github/workflows/bench.yml` on PHP 8.4.27 in place of the PHP 8.5.10 record of 2026-10-02: the native extension of template 0.0.20 renders 1,000 rows in 29.6 ms and the generated program in 15.2 ms, and the SSR script and the CSR shell exceed their size limits (H26.9).
- make bench-server builds the assets, which a clean checkout lacked, so every request answered status 500; the server benchmark fails when a request answers another status than the one it measures (H26.9-2).
- `.github/workflows/bench.yml` runs make bench and make bench-sessions one after another in one job on a manual run and uploads the report of each target, outside the checks and the release. The server benchmark sends a CSRF token with `POST /board/create`, so the request answers status 422, and the browser benchmark prints page errors, console errors and failed requests (H26.9-1).

## 0.0.29
- Release 0.0.29 after H27.72, H27.73, H27.74 and H27.76: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.29 (H27.75).
- A Node or Python request that changed nothing in a Redis session sets its new expiry without comparing the lock, as phpredis does, so a long request that only reads no longer answers status 500 when its lock expires; tests check a lock that really expires, a killed process, the HTTP servers of the board and a stop and a start of the Redis server (H27.72).
- The Redis sessions hold on a primary with replicas: RedisSentinelSessions and the sessionSetup option of App::open let the PHP server keep the session module of phpredis on the primary that Redis Sentinel names, the Node and Python servers follow it through a Sentinel client, and tests kill the primary under the three servers; the connections take TLS and an ACL user, also with Sentinel, and make bench-sessions measures the capacity of one Redis server (H27.73).
- The Redis sessions hold on Redis Cluster with their locks: RedisClusterSessions gives the PHP server the protocol of HY-115, which the rediscluster handler of phpredis lacks, and tests run the three servers on a real cluster of three primaries while a slot moves, through lock failures, a renewal, the HTTP servers, a replica that takes over, TLS and an ACL user (H27.74).
- Adopt kit 0.0.93, whose ci-setup installs the PHP symbol extractor, so the shared CI pipeline names no language (H27.76).

## 0.0.28
- Release 0.0.28 after H27.70: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.28 (H27.71).
- The PHP, Node and Python servers share the sessions of one Redis server with the protocol of phpredis 6.3.0: RedisSessions of the Node and Python servers keep the key PHPREDIS_SESSION:<id> under the lock <key>_LOCK, and a holder whose lock expired writes nothing (H27.70).
- The PHP, Node and Python servers share the sessions of one directory: a session is the file sess_<id> in the form php_serialize, each server holds flock on it from the opening of a session to its closing, the Node server through node:ffi and the Python and Node servers with the packages of the php-serialize release v0.0.1, and a value that a request did not set keeps its bytes (H27.69).
- make node-server bundles the board Node server with esbuild --platform=node --packages=external, so Node loads the packages of node_modules, where the bundle left only proper-lockfile external; the README of the Node server states that a Node server bundle of an application keeps its node_modules external (H27.68).

## 0.0.27
- Release 0.0.27 after H27.66: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.27 (H27.67).
- The requests of one session run one after another in every server process that shares the session directory: the Python server holds fcntl.flock on the lock file <id>.lock and the Node server the lock directory <id>.lock of proper-lockfile 4.1.2 from the opening to the closing of the session of a request, where both held a lock of their own process only, and four processes of 200 requests that each add one to a count of one session left 230 (Python) and 265 (Node) of 800. Only a missing session file starts a new session: the Python server started a new session with a new CSRF token for a session file that it could not decode, and now fails the request with the error. The removal of expired sessions skips a session whose lock a request holds, and FileSession.close of the Node server returns a promise (H27.66).

## 0.0.26
- Release 0.0.26 after H27.64 and H27.65: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.26; the release carries template 0.0.20 and kit 0.0.92.
- hyper pins template 0.0.20 by its asset names and digests: the wheel sha256 dbdeb77e...2e7b, the Composer zip polyspec-template-php-0.0.20.zip SHA-256 12c5eaf9...3da7 and SHA-1 a0e29731...68ff, and the npm tarballs by the sha512 of each released asset in package-lock.json and tests/package-install/package-lock.json (H27.65).
- H27.64: hyper adopts Kit 0.0.92 (H27.64).
## 0.0.25
- Release 0.0.25 after H27.57 to H27.62: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.25; the release carries template 0.0.19 and kit 0.0.88, and its Composer roots require the json-schema fork as min-0-max/json-schema.
- tests/package-install/package-lock.json pins the template 0.0.19 tarball by its own sha512 in place of the integrity of the 0.0.18 tarball, which npm kept when the URL changed, so make package-check installs it offline; template-pins.test.mts requires one integrity per asset across the npm locks (H27.63).
- hyper pins template 0.0.19 by its asset names and digests, and both Composer roots require the json-schema fork by its own name, "min-0-max/json-schema": "dev-polyspec-origin-2.6.0", in place of "opis/json-schema": "2.6.0-p1", since template 0.0.19 requires that name; the locks pin the default branch polyspec-origin-2.6.0 of the fork and hold no opis/json-schema, and AGENTS.md and development.md name that branch (H27.62).
- hyper adopts kit 0.0.88, whose make kit-gates-static runs every shared gate before a push and whose kit-check judges one CI pipeline (S12), no editable installs (S11) and no manifest path out of the repository (P30); make kit-sync writes its PRINCIPLES (H27.61).
- The job check of ci.yml follows the job kit with needs: kit, as kit S12 requires, so it runs only after the gates of kit passed (H27.60); the tests H27.24 and H27.25 accept that needs and still rejects any other.
- AGENTS.md names only checks that a target runs in the Enforced by line of its template pins rule, so rules-check passes, and the owner checks select rules-check for AGENTS.md and PRINCIPLES.md (H27.59). The line named the test file of template-pins in backticks, which rules-check reads as a check that no target reaches.
- The Composer development sources link only through the .links of kit and make link, and the release graph binds the opis/json-schema fork by its tag (H27.58). composer.local.json and examples/board/composer.local.json declared Composer path repositories to ../template/packages/php and ../forks/json-schema, a second link mechanism beside .links that the local links of kit forbid; they, make composer-development, composer-development-check, scripts/check-composer-development.mts and tests/scripts/composer-development.test.mts are removed. composer.json and examples/board/composer.json declare the VCS repository https://github.com/min-0-max/json-schema and require opis/json-schema 2.6.0-p1, and both locks moved from upstream 2.6.0 to the tag v2.6.0-p1 of the fork, which keeps the name opis/json-schema while the branch master of the fork carries the name min-0-max/json-schema, so CI and the release run the template decoder whose collect-all-errors mode no longer reports valid properties under additionalProperties: false and runs unevaluatedProperties after a failing group.
- CI checks hyper out at the workspace root and checks every template pin in place of a template checkout (H27.57). The jobs check and ci-passed of .github/workflows/ci.yml run uses: ./.github/actions/kit-bootstrap right after their checkout, in place of their own setup-node, make bootstrap, make install-tools TOOLS=npm and make install-packages, and no job checks out polyspec/template or runs in the path hyper. The Makefile has no TEMPLATE_REPOSITORY and no template-tag, make check no longer depends on it, and release-prepare runs only make hyper-install. tests/scripts/template-pins.test.mts checks the template release in each manifest and its integrity in each lock: the sha512 of the npm locks, the sha1 of the Composer locks and the sha256 of pyproject.toml and uv.lock. packages/server/python/pylock.toml and constraints.txt, which no recipe, test, workflow or release step read, are deleted; uv.lock is the lock of the Python package.

## 0.0.24
- Release 0.0.24 after H27.55 and H27.56: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote hold 0.0.24; the release carries template 0.0.18 and Kit 0.0.87.
- Kit 0.0.87 is the pinned release (H27.55). make kit-sync vendors its bootstrap action .github/actions/kit-bootstrap/action.yml, which the copied ci-pipeline.yml and release.yml run in every job (K992); a make run follows the linked kit after install-link replaces the packed one (K993).
- Template 0.0.18 is the pinned release (H27.56): TEMPLATE_VERSION, the CI checkout, the manifests and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote name it, with the digests of its wheel and its Composer zip.

## 0.0.23
- Release 0.0.23 after H27.53 and H27.54: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and their locks hold 0.0.23, so the release carries template 0.0.17 and Kit 0.0.86; its version commit is the tip of its push, whose green CI releases it (K987).

- Template 0.0.17 is the pinned release (H27.53): TEMPLATE_VERSION, the CI checkout, the manifests and the locks that npm, Composer and uv wrote name it. Its npm tarballs and wheel keep the names of their packers, polyspec-template-0.0.17.tgz, polyspec-template-compiler-0.0.17.tgz and polyspec_template-0.0.17-py3-none-any.whl, in place of the names with the runtime, and the Composer locks take the zip polyspec-template-php-0.0.17.zip from the dist URL of packages.json; template-version, template-dir and template-pins of tests/scripts expect these names. docs/operations/development.md names the neighbours that the native resolution test refuses, 0.0.16 and 0.0.18.
- Kit 0.0.86 is the pinned release (H27.54). package-check and hyper-server-php-install link with make install-link PROJECT=tests/package-install and PROJECT=examples/board, which link that consumer alone and write no Claude Code hook (K983); make install-link of the whole tree started uv for the Python workspace and failed in the CI node job, which has no uv. The release workflow starts by itself when the CI run of a push whose tip is the version-bump commit succeeds (K987), which AGENTS.md, docs/operations/development.md and HR-95 name.
- Kit 0.0.85 is the pinned release (H27.50), by its asset polyspec-kit-0.0.85.tgz. A release is dispatched with the id of its CI run alone and takes its version from the first `## X.Y.Z` of the change log (K924), so tests/scripts/ci-workflow.test.mts expects no version input and the docs name `gh workflow run release.yml -f run=<id>`. npm is the `npm:npm` 12.2.0 of mise.toml with its digests in mise.lock, which devEngines of package.json names in place of packageManager (K938), and make bootstrap runs it outside the checkout with --prefix. config/release.json declares the product and the runtime of each package, whose asset keeps the name its packer gives and carries the label `<vendor>-<product>-<runtime>-<version>` (P39, K980). hyper-server-php-install and package-check link with make install-link, which writes no Claude Code hook, in place of make link, which writes it since K967 and so failed in an installed kit.
- Hyper 0.0.22 is released at 51d8cbb after CI 41039924973 and gates 41039924928: the release run 41041100350 published the six assets, and the release carries template 0.0.15 and Kit 0.0.78 (H27.48).
- Kit 0.0.79 is the pinned release (H27.49): the job `kit` of `.github/workflows/ci.yml` calls `.github/workflows/ci-pipeline.yml`, which kit-sync copies in place of the removed gates workflow, so one CI run holds the gates of kit and the checks of hyper, and `ci-passed` needs `kit`; `config/release.json` declares no `checks`, and a release is `gh workflow run release.yml -f run=<id> -f version=X.Y.Z`, whose `make release-tag` creates and pushes `vX.Y.Z` at the commit of that CI run; `make template-ext` builds the native template extension with the pie-build tool of kit in an empty `var/pie/polyspec/template-php-ext` (K915), and the CI entries and pipeline phases that run `make hyper-install-php` install `pie` with `make install-tools`.
- `make template-ext` runs PIE in its own recipe: it empties `var/pie/polyspec/template-php-ext`, runs `pie repository:add composer` of the Composer repository of the template release and `pie build polyspec/template-php-ext:TEMPLATE_VERSION` with that folder as `PIE_WORKING_DIRECTORY` and `--no-interaction`, and publishes the one library that `scripts/pie-binary.mts` finds recorded for the package in its `installed.json`, failing when PIE recorded none or more than one; it no longer runs the pie-build tool of kit, which kit 0.0.81 removes (K931) (H27.51).
- `config/rebuild.json` classes `scripts/pie-binary.mts` as `ecosystem`, since it only reads the `installed.json` that PIE and Composer write, so `make rebuild-check` passes again on main, where CI run 41096921017 failed on the missing class (H27.52).

## 0.0.22
- Kit 0.0.78 is the pinned release (H27.48-1): make link of an installed kit writes no Claude Code hook (K916), so `make link PROJECT=tests/package-install` of package-check passes in CI, where the installed kit has no node of its own var/tools and no `.links` names a provider.
- The CI setup of every runtime is the one that `config/ci.json` declares in `setup`, so kit's ci-setup runs no default `deps-<runtime>` target and the Makefile defines none (H27.47).
- Release 0.0.22 after H27.47-3: every manifest of config/release.json, the exact requirements of polyspec/hyper-server and @polyspec/hyper-client and their locks hold 0.0.22, so the release carries template 0.0.15 from its Composer repository and Kit 0.0.78. (H27.48)
- The docs, php and python jobs of `.github/workflows/ci.yml` no longer install phpcs, phpstan, ruff, alint and vulture, which only the gates of kit start and `.github/workflows/gates.yml` runs (kit K870), and the php job no longer installs the PHP symbol extractor, which none of its targets reads; ci-workflow fails for a job that selects a lint tool of kit. (H27.47-3)
- Template 0.0.15 is the pinned release (H27.47-2): the npm tarballs, the Composer repository packages.json with the zip polyspec-template-php-0.0.15.zip, the wheel with its sha256 and the native extension that PIE builds come from the release v0.0.15, and npm, Composer and uv wrote their locks; TEMPLATE_VERSION and the CI checkout of the template repository name it. Each template release has its own working directory of PIE, var/pie/<TEMPLATE_VERSION>: PIE keeps the repositories that it added in that directory, and the repository of v0.0.14 hid 0.0.15, so pie build found no installable polyspec/template-php-ext 0.0.15.
- Kit 0.0.77 is the pinned release (H27.47-2). The recipes run no toolchain check, since kit deleted it (K854): a missing tool fails the target that runs it. make hyper-install-python installs through make uv-install (K850); make composer-development runs composer install with COMPOSER=composer.local.json and make package-check runs npm ci of tests/package-install, each followed by make link, since kit deleted its install wrappers (K859). The git-ignored .links declares the provider checkouts (K869), and the prepare-commit-msg hook, whose target kit deleted, is gone (K892). `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `CHECK_TARGETS` no longer run the gates of kit, which `.github/workflows/gates.yml` runs (K870): the language-parity job goes, and protocol parity runs in the parity job of gates.yml. The documents describe make ext as the check of the library that PIE built and the Composer repositories of the releases as the install of a consumer (K865).

## 0.0.21
- The hyper-install case of the template-ext test judges the recursive make of template-ext by its words, a make by its base name and template-ext as its last word: it matched a slash before make, which only a make started by its path prints, and failed in CI, which prints the bare name make. (H27.45-2)
- make template-ext installs http:pie of mise.toml with mise install --locked before it runs PIE: a CI job installs only the TOOLS of its matrix and install-tools of kit has no pie selector, so make hyper-install-php found no shim of PIE in CI. The template-ext test asserts the install and judges a pie install by the PIE command only, not by the path of the checkout. (H27.45-1)
- Release 0.0.21 after H27.45: the entries of ## Unreleased since the release commit of H27.29 move into ## 0.0.21, with a new empty ## Unreleased above it, so the release carries template 0.0.14 from its Composer repository and Kit 0.0.75. (H27.46)
- Kit 0.0.75 is the pinned release: a worktree installs its own tools and packages online with make install, and make worktree-setup installs and builds nothing itself (kit K857, K858); the release writes packages.json of the Composer packages beside the archives (kit K871). The tests judge the tools of every checkout by its own var/tools (tests/scripts/checkout-tools.mts replaces main-tools.mts), and the toolchain case judges make template-ext, which runs PHP and Composer, in place of make ext, which since H27.45 only requires the built library. (H27.47-1)
- Template 0.0.14 is the pinned release, and hyper reads it from the Composer repository of the release, its packages.json: composer.json and examples/board/composer.json declare it in place of the package repository that restated the zip, and make hyper-install builds the native extension polyspec/template-php-ext of it with PIE 1.4.10 of mise.toml (make template-ext) in var/pie, so config/release-assets.json, release-assets-fetch and scripts/build-template-ext.mts are gone and make ext only requires the built library; the native Composer test reads the supported version from packages/server/php/composer.json. (H27.45)
- Release 0.0.21: every manifest of config/release.json holds 0.0.21, and the entries of ## Unreleased move into ## 0.0.21, with a new empty ## Unreleased above it. (H27.29)
- Template 0.0.13 is the pinned release of the npm, Composer, Python and php-ext dependencies, the template tag and the CI checkout. Composer fetches the php zip itself from a package repository of composer.json and examples/board/composer.json that names its URL and sha1, so config/release-assets.json pins only the php-ext archive and TEMPLATE_TAG reads its version; the package restates the metadata of the zip, which no check compares with the release. The test of the hyper-install recipe reads the recipe of the main checkout and its tools, so it gives the same result in a worktree. (H27.44)
- Kit 0.0.72 is the pinned release: CI links nothing (kit K824), so the gates workflow runs no make link and link-state-check leaves the CI suite; it checks the links of a development checkout. (H27.43)
- The docs job of CI installs the Python environment of the server package, since public-symbols of hyper-test-scripts reads the installed distribution with the python extractor of kit (kit K788); it extracted the symbols of two of the three implementations. (H27.42)
- The toolchain test expects make hyper-install-python to make the venv anew and run uv sync --no-editable, as H27.39 changed it. (H27.41)
- config/parity.json declares each Python symbol by its import path, polyspec.hyper.<module>.<name>, which the python extractor of kit reads since kit K788. (H27.40)
- make hyper-install-python makes the venv anew and installs the Python server package non-editable, and the link of kit makes its package folder a link to the checkout: the python extractor of kit gave no symbols for the editable install (kit K788), and link-state-check found no installed package folder. (H27.39)
- Kit 0.0.70 is the pinned release: its gates workflow passes no commit range, and AGENTS and the documents state that make docs-check reads each document on its own, with no comparison of the twins (kit K809), that make kit-sync writes the copies and that no guard refuses a local make check. (H27.38)
- AGENTS no longer names make kit-check as the check of the copies of kit, and a test comment no longer states that it compares them; kit deleted that comparison (kit K799), and make kit-sync refreshes the copies from the installed package. (H27.37)
- The release workflow, which kit-sync writes, has no make release-versions step, and the rules and documents no longer describe the version and manifest checks of a release that kit deletes (kit K812); the package managers resolve the versions. (H27.36)
- The ignored-files test no longer names var/full-run.json and var/full-run.lock, the outputs of the full-run guard that kit deleted (kit K240). (H27.35)
- A release builds and publishes its archives without a consumer install or a proof: config/release.json has no consumers or proof, tests/release-install and the npm allow-remote settings of release-consumer are gone, and a consumer checks a release with its own package manager (kit K798). A push is not checked: .githooks/pre-push, the hook of config/checklist.json and push-gate-commit of the suite are gone (kit K804). (H27.33)
- make hooks writes the pre-commit hook of kit again, and the pre-merge-commit hook, config/commit-identity.json and commits-check of the suite, which kit deletes (K805), are gone. (H27.34)

- Remove pythonBackend of config/toolchain.json, consumers.wheel.polyspec of config/release.json and config/dependency-policy.json with its owner; the wheel builds with the build backend of build-system.requires, and uv pip compile writes pylock.toml. (H27.31)
- make hooks writes the hooks of kit again and the reference-transaction hook, which kit deletes (K801), leaves .githooks: main moves through make land and through a commit whose hooks passed. (H27.32)
- The release-prepare case of the template version tests matches the recursive make of hyper-install by the target that ends its line, since $(MAKE) of kit-bootstrap.mk adds KIT and KIT_EXT to every recursive make. (H27.30)
- Remove dependency-policy-check, dependency-policy-mutation-check and the review record config/dependency-review.json from the suite, the CI matrix, the owners and the rules; config/dependency-policy.json stays for the Python lock that make python-lock writes. (H27.28)
- Adopt the published integrity-locked Kit 0.0.65, which carries the Playwright run of the shared test runner and whose rules check reaches every runtime of the gates matrix, and synchronize its copies. (H27.27)
- The owner tests name each kit tool by the real path of the installed kit, from which make runs it in a linked checkout and a packed install alike. (H27.27-1)
- Pin shared tools 0.0.63 for native bootstrap and runtime-scoped source linking. (H27.26)
- Run every browser case of `make e2e` on servers of the run through the Playwright run of the shared test runner, which reports each case with its own timeout; `CASES=<pattern>` selects cases. (H27.26)
- Require both application CI and the provider gate aggregate to succeed on the tagged commit before publication.
- Adopt Kit 0.0.62 and declare native shared, runtime and full-parity gate setup. Keep the copied provider workflow unchanged and preserve every mandatory gate.
- Delegate Python unit, package-data, rendering and scoped multipart checks to the installed shared runner. Remove the private unittest harness; native reporting, per-test timeouts and empty-selection failures cover each execution.

## 0.0.20
- Select template source checkouts independently from Composer installation. Common policy checks retain their locked release packages without discovering an unrelated development source. (H27.25-1)
- Verify all five 0.0.20 archives in isolated native consumers without development links, alongside the selective-install workflow owner checks. (H27.25)
- Adopt the published Kit 0.0.61 selective installer. Bootstrap npm separately, select only each CI group’s native tools, explicitly install required PHP symbol extractors, and keep language and comparison environments independent.
- Declare per-job tool selections and separate locked Composer and Python installations. Language builds validate only their own tools; common script checks and cross-server comparisons execute in the groups that own all required environments.
- Run protocol declaration validation and cross-language equivalence in an independent language-parity CI job, with its own report and required final result, without browser installation. (H27.24) (H27.25)

## 0.0.19
- Record all six passing application CI groups on PHP 8.4.27 and the passing policy gates in feature status; distinguish application CI runners from shared workflow configuration. (H27.23)
- Bind the Python file-limit parity case to its rendering test and require a present native-install command before inspecting its offline policy. (H27.22-1)
- Verify that the package consumer delegates installation to native npm while retaining the offline recipe environment, rather than requiring an inline npm command. (H27.21-1)
- Discover multipart contract cases in the pure Python suite and application limits in the rendering suite, whose target builds the server program first. (H27.22)
- Classify the read-only Composer source-consistency checker as application policy and declare its mandatory CI unit-test target as the enforcement path. (H27.16-1)
- Script checks build their asset and server inputs explicitly and select archive-fetch tools through installed exports. Package checks delegate locked installation and source links to native npm without editor-hook setup. (H27.21)
- Every CI check group prepares the declared PHP before tool discovery and installs its native Git hook configuration before validation. (H27.20)
- Native gates build published workspace declarations and runtime fixtures before reading them. PHP test fixtures retain their behavior while satisfying the configured line-length limit. (H27.19)
- Explicit bootstrap installation enables its declared downloads even when the caller exports npm offline mode; the remaining checks retain their offline environment. (H27.15-1)
- Verify the 0.0.19 npm, Composer and wheel archives in isolated consumers without development links (H27.3).
- Resolve the published template 0.0.12 archives and shared tools 0.0.60 through native package managers, regenerate the release locks for PHP 8.4 and align the Python wheel lock. Keep the PHP library minimum >=8.4 and source development declarations separate. (H27.2)
- Explicit Composer development manifests resolve source template 0.0.12 and the existing JSON Schema fork through native path repositories, with separate local locks and accurate installed records. Preserve the native COMPOSER selector, declare the library compatibility alternatives and validate both development graphs on PHP 8.4. Native graph checks, 18 related tool tests and 87 PHP application tests pass; release locks remain unchanged (H27.16).
- TypeScript lint resolves its tool executables from the installed provider dependency packages and locates the native type-aware backend without application-created bin links (H27.17).
- The native source-linked TypeScript lint passes oxlint, knip, six TypeScript projects and catch-check; all nine steps execute successfully (H27.17).
- Fresh checkouts bootstrap the lock through the declared native npm before reading shared tools from the installed package. CI uses that entry, and owner tests resolve source or packed tools from installed exports without mandatory sibling paths. A standalone locked archive fixture and the scoped integration checks pass (H27.15).
- Adopt the shared-tool archive 0.0.56 through npm with its release URL and integrity. Refresh the native dependency review and synchronized shared copy while preserving installed source links. The shared-tool, hook, dependency-policy and link checks pass (H27.14).
- The PHP 8.4 benchmark retry depends on an idle host rather than a separate phase instruction. The current host sample is 55.41 percent idle, so the idle-run requirement remains open (H26.9).
- The Darwin virtiofs tests mount resolved installed-source provider checkouts read-only at their native paths. The image remains digest-pinned and is installed explicitly with hyper-install-virtiofs; checks reject a missing local image before running a container. Both native virtiofs tests pass (H27.13).
- PHP request maps expose native iteration without internal storage return types; uploaded files now appear in foreach, retaining request order. Public upload metadata and file-limit declarations match every server, response header signatures expose no private alias, and the file-parts binding executes its actual contract test. Native API symbol checks, PHP regressions and scoped file-parts parity pass Grouping assertions retain their precise inferred types (H27.12).
- Remove the unused filesystem import from the installed-source assertions; TypeScript lint checks pass (H27.8-1).
- The cleanup-timeout audit found a 55-second host suspension overlapping the 30-second SIGTERM test deadline. The unchanged four cleanup tests pass after resume; preserve the original failure and power-event evidence without claiming a code repair or extending the deadline (H27.9).
- Final verification invokes the current native kit report runner with the complete platform target set. Procedures preserve the single final attempt and separate failed-owner reports instead of calling a removed entry point (H27.11).
- Synchronize tracked shared files and workflows as regular installed-package copies and refresh the repository hooks. Installed package source links remain in place; shared-copy and hook checks pass (H27.10).
- The server benchmark reports the generated PHP program and template extension state without reading a removed Renderer property. An isolated benchmark regression rejects PHP warnings and verifies database removal (H27.7).
- Installed-source tests verify the resolved template entry and shared module identity while release declarations retain their archive pins. PHP analysis assertions reject extension stubs; all seven targeted tests pass (H27.8).
- The PHP exact public API assertion includes UploadedFile and recognizes native traits when checking autoloadable declarations. The API test and PHP static analysis pass (H27.6-1).
- PHP route and request-copy contracts retain their precise types with valid PHPDocs. Fixtures validate JSON input and callback responses, and server startup transfers ownership only after success while cleaning up failures. PHPStan reports no errors, formatting checks pass, and 116 related tests pass (H27.5).
- Exact public API checks include the existing TypeScript UploadedFile export and Python uploaded_file module of HY-113. The Python root exports stay unchanged; both native API checks pass (H27.6).
- Native npm source linking uses direct-dependency override references without EOVERRIDE. The package fixture receives the pinned npm path, its generated link state is ignored, and the ignore check verifies Git succeeded and checked every output. Package installation, source links and the targeted regressions pass (H27.4).
- Separate the prepared local candidate from pending archive-consumer proof: the latter awaits compatible distribution inputs and the archive target tag, while local final verification can proceed (H27.3).
- The board server build uses installed source packages without Composer installation or relinking. Explicit dependency installation remains available. The regression test and the 11-template server build pass (H27.3-1).
- The 0.0.19 candidate passes linked-source unit checks: 8 package-source/name tests, 71 PHP application tests on PHP 8.4.26 both without and with the template extension loaded, 76 TypeScript application tests, 4 client stream tests, and 19 Python application plus 9 multipart tests. This evidence covers local source use; archive installation and full-suite verification remain open (H27.3).
- Native npm audit updates fast-uri to 3.1.9 and markdown-it to 14.3.3 in the candidate lock; remove the advisory exception no longer present in the audit (H27.3).
- Prepare the 0.0.19 manifests, five candidate archives and native consumer installation locks (H27.3).
- The release-readiness audit retains the four withdrawn rows as history, restores Composer resolution and lock validation in H27.2, adds clean archive verification in H27.3, and keeps the idle benchmark H26.9 open (H27.1).
- H25.73 is withdrawn because reconciling tracked locks with linked development manifests contradicts H26.8. PHP 8.4 remains the runtime target, and the locks are unchanged (H25.73).
- The committed composition documentation passes parity-declarations-check and docs-check (H25.76).
- Application authoring rules HC-1 to HC-7 remain mandatory in docs/operations/composition.md. HY-48 states the rejection of hx-* attributes outside the layout explicitly; protocol requirement tracing passes (H25.76).
- H26.4 records server file-part parity separately from the client stream requirements HYC-17 to HYC-21; its stale pending-client clause is removed (H26.4-1).
- The stale-text check passes on the committed H26.4 correction (H26.4-1).
- The release assets test checks that PHPStan reads no stub of the template extension, as H25.60 left it (H25.82).
- The setup-php test accepts the .php-version of the checkout folder of the gates workflow (H25.81).
- The matrix of ci.yml runs suppression-check, so the CI run of the full suite runs every target of CHECK_TARGETS once (H25.80).
- The CI workflow and release assets tests expect the kit sources that hyper runs since kit K613 (H25.79).
- The toolchain test expects the sources of ../kit that hyper runs since kit K613, and the gates workflow installs the sibling kit without a build (H25.78).
- The repository holds no copy of the kit bootstrap or of PRINCIPLES: the Makefile includes the kit-bootstrap.mk of ../kit, scripts/kit and config/php-lint are gone, and PRINCIPLES.md, its twin, .editorconfig and .claude/settings.json are links to ../kit (kit K614) (H25.77).
- The Git hooks of kit are tracked and installed, pre-merge-commit, prepare-commit-msg and reference-transaction among them, so the hooks that PRINCIPLES names exist, and the copies of kit follow the linked kit (H25.75).
- No catch of hyper accepts every failure: the eight catches without a binding bind their error, the renders mark any Error with hy-error (HYC-14) and let a value that is no Error propagate, and the reads of a kept text, of the keep cookie and of strict UTF-8 take only their own failure (BindError, URIError, TypeError) (H25.74).
- H25.72-2: tests/scripts/toolchain.test.mts judges make hyper-install by the dry run of the main checkout, in a worktree as on main, since a worktree installs no tools (kit K519), and expects install-tools.mjs before the first check of the toolchain, the order of install-kit of kit. - H25.25: The Node server implements two rules that the specification states for every server. `ServerOptions.around` (HY-97) calls a hook once for every request with the request and `answer`; `answer` opens the session, answers with the application and adds the cookie of a new session, and `Request.withHeader` sets a header of a copy. A response of `around` alone opens no session and runs no loader, action or hook; an error of `around` and a value that is no `Response` are escaped errors with the plain 500 and the write hook; the disconnect hook receives the request and the reply of the last call of `answer`. ## 0.0.18
- H23.29: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.53.
- H23.24: the board example loads the template decoder with `import()` when it fetches its first template, so a server-rendered page does not load it, and `make bundle-size` measures the chunk files of the static deployment as the output `chunks`. The limits of `config/bundle-size.json` are measured again with the template decoder and the protocol code of the client: 35,900 gzip bytes for the SSR script, 36,000 for the CSR shell and 7,700 for the chunks.
- H23.28: hyper depends on template 0.0.11, which adds the entry `@polyspec/template/decode` with a smaller browser decoder; a generated PHP program renders a block whose argument has no root value and fails on a `define` option that is not an object with the argument error of the language.
- H23.27: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.52. `.php-version` declares the PHP 8.5 that every workflow sets up, and the release workflow runs `make kit-check` before any release step (kit K193).

## 0.0.17
- H23.24: the board example builds a client script whose gzip size, 43,933 bytes, and a static shell, 44,081 bytes, exceed the limits 33,500 and 33,600 of `config/bundle-size.json`, which `make bundle-size` reports as warnings: the browser checks every template that it fetches with `decodeTemplate`, whose validator `@polyspec/template` 0.0.9 bundles whole. They stay over the limits until a template release gives the browser a smaller decoder.
- H23.25: hyper depends on template 0.0.9, whose generated Rust and TypeScript programs declare only what they use; the PHP program, the native extension and the Python package that hyper uses are unchanged.
- H23.13: `@polyspec/hyper-server` reads each template AST of the template root with `decodeTemplate` of `@polyspec/template` 0.0.8, which checks it against the AST schema, so a template file that is not a template AST fails with the path of its first invalid node instead of rendering; the board client and `scripts/check-parity.mts` read the template files the same way. `make lint-ts` passes and runs in `make check` and in the CI job `docs`.
- H23.23: hyper depends on template 0.0.8. `hyper-build-server` of `@polyspec/hyper-build` writes the generated PHP program as PSR-4 files, one class per file, into the directory `php` of the server program in place of `program.php`, because the compiler of template 0.0.8 writes the PHP target only that way. The PHP server loads the class `GeneratedProgram` of the namespace of `program.json` through the autoload of Composer: an application maps that namespace to the directory `php` in `autoload.psr-4` of its `composer.json`, and `App::open` fails with the class and the directory to map when the autoload does not load it, or with the path of the missing directory `php` (HY-48). The Python server package requires `jsonschema` 4.26.0 through the template wheel, and `polyspec/template` 0.0.8 brings `opis/json-schema` and `symfony/console` into a Composer install.
- H23.22: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.50. knip reads the binaries that the scripts and tests run from `binaries` of `config/knip-entries.json`, and kit's `knip.json` ignores no binary (kit K176); the bootstrap defines `$(NODE)` before kit is installed (K185).

## 0.0.16
- H23.20: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.48. Each checkout runs the Node.js 26.12.0 of `mise.toml`, which the bootstrap installs into `var/tools`, in place of `.node-version`; `engines.node` of the packages is `>=26.12.0`, and the workflows pin their actions by SHA (kit K169, K171).
- H23.19: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.47, whose oxlint fails every non-null assertion and applies the type-checked rules to the TypeScript files only (kit K156, K170).
- H23.13: the TypeScript passes the type-checked rules of kit 0.0.46 and 0.0.47 (K155, K156, K157) without casts, `any` or non-null assertions. `@polyspec/hyper-client`: `checkManifest` takes the manifest as `unknown` and returns the declarations that it checked, and the new `checkTemplateIndex` checks a template index the same way; `Hyper`, `HtmxApi` and `HyperOptions` are generic over the region element (`Element` by default), `HtmxApi` has `find`, which the default `element` option uses, and `parseInterval` returns `number | undefined`, where undefined is no timeout, so the htmx of the page fits `HtmxApi` as it is; the types `RegionElement` and `AssignmentElement` are exported. `@polyspec/hyper-server`: `Services` binds only own keys, so a key of `Object.prototype` such as `constructor` has no factory; a session whose stored flash or kept values are not a map of values, or whose file holds a key that is not a string, fails with the file instead of passing the values on.
- H23.18: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.46. The wheel build backend is declared once, as `pythonBackend` of `config/toolchain.json` (kit K166), and kit covers the root `composer.json` and `composer.lock` in the layout and `config/php-lint/composer.json` in the release coverage (K167).

## 0.0.15
- H23.17: the tag v0.0.14 has no GitHub Release, because its release workflow failed before it published: the wheel build backend was missing from `var/tools/wheels`. Release 0.0.15 contains the changes of 0.0.14 and the declaration of the backend below.
- H23.17: `config/toolchain.json` declares the wheel build backend `setuptools==84.0.0` with its sha256 as `pythonBackend`, so `make install-tools`, which the release workflow runs through `make release-tools`, installs it into `var/tools/wheels`; the release workflow of the tag v0.0.14 failed because the wheel was missing.

- H23.14: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.44 with the integrity of the published file; its copies keep the cache of ruff in `var/ruff`, give the bootstrap bytes as `Uint8Array` and let `config/layout.yml` add root files to the list of kit, and `make install-tools` removes the wrappers of `var/tools/bin` that kit no longer writes (kit K145, K147, K149, K150).
- H23.13: `valueToJson` of `@polyspec/hyper-client` writes a safe string as its text, where it wrote `"[object Object]"`, and an application object fails as on the server; the `fetch` option of the client is the exported type `Fetch`, which takes the URL as a string, as the client calls it. The findings of `make lint-ts` in this repository are fixed; the check stays out of the full suite until the kit release with K145 and K150.
- H23.12: `make dependency-review UPDATE=1` reviewed the lock of kit 0.0.43 and raised `@playwright/test` to 1.64.1 with its lock.
- H23.6: `make typecheck` builds the npm packages first, since `scripts/check-parity.mts` imports the declarations of `@polyspec/hyper-client` from its dist; the docs job of CI, which builds no package, failed with TS2307.
- H23.12: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.43 with the integrity of the published file; its copies let oxlint allow the registrations of node:test, give knip the scripts, benchmarks and test files as entries, and turn `ae-missing-release-tag` of API Extractor off (kit K138, K139, K142).
- H23.6: the folders follow S5, S7 and S9 of kit's layout, and `make layout-check` passes. The packages move to `packages/client`, `packages/build` and `packages/server/{ts,php,python}`, with their published names unchanged; `conformance/` is `contracts/`; `playwright.config.ts` is in `tests/e2e`; the benchmarks are in `benchmarks/`. The scripts and tool tests are TypeScript (`.mts`) that `make typecheck` checks with the root `tsconfig.json`. No recipe sets `PYTHONPATH`: `make install-python` makes the venv of the Python server package with `uv sync --locked` from its `uv.lock`, and the board Python server runs its Python, which the Makefile exports as `HYPER_PYTHON`. Composer installs the PHP server package from the path repository `packages/server/php` with `symlink: false`, and `make hyper-server-php-install` mirrors it into the board again; `scripts/copy-package.mjs` is removed.

The test that the installed copy holds no `src` became a test of the files that `npm pack --dry-run` lists for each package; the released archives stay covered by `make release-consumer`.
- H23.4: the development dependencies are at their latest stable releases: `vitest` 5.0.4, `laravel/pint` 1.32.1 and `phpunit/phpunit` 13.4.2 in the root `composer.json`, `packages/hyper-server-php/composer.json` and `examples/board/composer.json`; the pins below the releases that require PHP 8.3 are gone. The data sets of `ResultTest` are named `0x` and the hex of the location, because PHPUnit 13 refuses the empty key of the empty location.
- H23.10: hyper pins `@polyspec/kit` 0.0.42 with the integrity of the published file; its bootstrap writes `var/tools/go.mod`, so a Go that mise installs under `var/tools` is a module of its own (kit K135).
- H23.3: the shared tools are the npm package `@polyspec/kit`, pinned by the URL of its release asset and its integrity. The vendored `scripts/kit/`, `tests/kit/`, `.kit/kit.lock.json` and `kit.json` are removed and `make kit-test` with them; the Makefile includes `scripts/kit/kit-bootstrap.mk` and runs the built tools of the package, a checkout starts with `make install-tools TOOLS=npm` and `make install-packages`, and every CI job runs both first. `make kit-sync` writes the copies of `kit.copies`, `config/layout.yml` maps the folders for `make layout-check`, `config/toolchain.json` declares uv and alint, `.npmrc` turns the audit of `npm ci` off, and the pre-push hook runs `make push-gate-hook`. This repository has no `go.mod` and no nested ruff configuration.

- H23.2: npm installs with its own defaults. `.npmrc` no longer sets `install-links=true` and `bin-links=false`; the root `package.json` declares the npm workspaces `packages/*` (`packages/hyper-client`, `packages/hyper-server-node` and `packages/hyper-build`), the packages build with the `tsc` of the bin link of npm, and the recipes start TypeScript and Playwright through `node_modules/.bin`. `scripts/tsc.mjs` and the `npm-copy` action of `scripts/publish.mjs` are removed, and `tests/scripts/no-symlinks.test.mjs` checks the Composer `vendor` directories only.
- H23.1: the toolchain floors are the pins of kit: `.node-version` is 26.11.1 and `engines.node` of every `package.json` is `>=26.11.1`; the root `composer.json`, `packages/hyper-server-php/composer.json` and `examples/board/composer.json` require PHP `>=8.5` on the Composer platform 8.5.0, which `config/dependency-policy.json` names, and their locks record it.
- H22.1: `AGENTS.md` and `AGENTS.ko.md` state that an existing outside tool or library is used as it is, with its own behavior, such as the symbolic links that it creates, and that the path and symbolic link rules govern what this repository creates. No tool configuration changed.
- H21.7: `make virtiofs-check` runs its two tests again: the guard of `tests/virtiofs/output-files.test.mjs` checks the template package that the build resolves, not the removed declared copy, so the run no longer fails with `ReferenceError: template is not defined`. The target stays on Darwin only, and the description of the target and the dependency row of the container image state why and name the test that covers the cause on every platform.
- H21.6: the helper `make` of `tests/scripts/check-recipes.test.mjs` runs `make` without `FORCE_COLOR`, so its test of the type check of `make test-js` passes in a shell that sets `FORCE_COLOR`. tsc colours its output whenever `FORCE_COLOR` is set, which broke the match of `wrong.ts(1,14): error TS2322`; `NO_COLOR` is kept.

The npm packages `@polyspec/template` and `@polyspec/template-compiler` are installed from the tarballs of the release v0.0.6 in the root `package.json` (dependencies and overrides), `packages/hyper-build`, `packages/hyper-client`, `packages/hyper-server-node` and `tests/package-install`, and their locks are written by npm. Composer installs `polyspec/template` 0.0.6 from its zip in the root `composer.json`, `packages/hyper-server-php/composer.json` and `examples/board/composer.json`, and their locks are written by Composer. The zip has the sha1 `02f0244bd3fd663b68576d0c740284306b3f8320`, and the php-ext asset `polyspec-template-php-ext-php-0.0.6.zip` has the sha256 `0e6253200c7314f7ef89b3ebb5f90a983331795ca49dd177fb70511e0dde5092`; both match the digests of the release assets. `config/template-ext.json`, `TEMPLATE_TAG` of the Makefile, the template checkouts of the workflows and the Python git tag of `packages/hyper-server-python/pyproject.toml` name v0.0.6. The consumer projects of `tests/release-install` keep 0.0.5, because the archives of the hyper release 0.0.13 require 0.0.5 exactly. The changes of the template between 0.0.5 and 0.0.6 that hyper uses are the version fields of the npm, Composer and php-ext packages, and the Python `requires-python` `>=3.14`, which `packages/hyper-server-python/pyproject.toml` already declares. `tests/scripts/template-version.test.mjs` checks every pin: seven of its eight cases fail on the 0.0.5 tree and all eight pass on 0.0.6.

## 0.0.13
- H21.3: the consumer projects of `tests/release-install` require the release 0.0.13: their manifests and locks name the archives `polyspec-hyper-client-npm-0.0.13.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-server-npm-0.0.13.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-build-npm-0.0.13.tgz` and `polyspec-hyper-server-php-0.0.13.zip`, and `make release-consumer TAG=v0.0.13` installs and checks them (#H21.3).
- H20.13: the pre-push hook checks the messages of every pushed commit with `config/commits.json` and refuses a push whose commit has a finding, so a commit with a body line over 72 characters is not pushed. The vendored kit is `v0.0.17`.
- H21.1: the reply gets the named method `referrerPolicy(value)` in PHP and Node and `referrer_policy(value)` in Python. It sets the `Referrer-Policy` of the page and JSON answers of the request, whatever the status of the answer, to one of the eight tokens of the Referrer-Policy specification; another value fails with HY-43 (HY-103).
- H20.12: `packages/hyper-server-python/pyproject.toml` declares `requires-python >=3.14`, the minor release of `.python-version` that CI tests (`tests/scripts/python-requires.test.mjs`). The dependency record states the same.
- H20.11: `make install-tools` installs the Python of `config/toolchain.json` `pythonBuild` (3.14.8) into `var/tools`, and every check and CI job runs that Python instead of the Python of PATH; the CI matrix entry `python-3.11` is removed, so CI runs the pinned release only. The release tag is an annotated tag (HY-95), and the vendored kit is `v0.0.16`.

## 0.0.12
- H20.10: the consumer projects of `tests/release-install` require the release 0.0.12: their manifests and locks name the archives `polyspec-hyper-client-npm-0.0.12.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-server-npm-0.0.12.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-build-npm-0.0.12.tgz` and `polyspec-hyper-server-php-0.0.12.zip`, and `make release-consumer TAG=v0.0.12` installs and checks them (#H20.10).
- H20.7: the write hook receives a null response for a request that ends before a response started: a closed connection (HY-67) in the Node server, an `exit()` without output in PHP, and a cancelled ASGI task before its answer. The hook gets `ended` false and no failure unless an error escaped (HY-99).
- H20.6: the PHP write hook follows what PHP sent under either output buffering mode. A fatal error with output buffering on drops the buffered body, so the plain 500 is the only body (HY-60, HY-99).
- H20.4: the Python server writes the escaped errors of its callables and of `create_server` with the failure of the error (HY-60, HY-99). A WSGI body is an iterable whose `close` reports a write that did not end, and a 204 or 304 response reports its write at the end of the iterable. A status, a header or a body that cannot be written, and a `start_response` that raises, are escaped errors that pass the plain 500 through `start_response` (HY-100). An ASGI cancellation of a send reports a write that did not end and propagates (HY-101). The write hook takes the failure as its fifth argument.
- H20.3: the Node server exposes its request listener as `App.listener(sessions, options)` (HY-102). The option `written` of `App.listener` and `App.server` calls the write hook once for every answered request after the response closes, before the disconnect hook, with `ended` equal to `writableFinished` (HY-99). The promise of the listener settles after both hooks. A request whose client closed the connection reports `ended` false with the plain 500 that it has not written.
- H20.2: the PHP server writes the plain 500 of an escaped error with the headers that the caller set before `App::run`, and no other header (HY-60). `App::open` takes the write hook `written`, which `App::run` calls once for every run, after the escape function and before the disconnect hook, with the response, `ended`, the elapsed time and the failure (HY-99). `ended` is false when the client closed the connection or when a fatal error followed the sent headers. The response hook receives no escaped 500.
- H20.1: the rules of the write hook and the plain 500 of an escaped error: HY-60 keeps the headers that the caller set before hyper received the request; HY-99 gives every server a write hook `written` with its request, response, `ended`, elapsed time and failure, called after the answer and the write and before the disconnect hook; HY-100 and HY-101 pass the escaped 500 to `start_response` or `send`; HY-102 names `App.listener` of the Node server. A PHP consumer no longer reads the write result from its own shutdown function, and no adapter reports an escaped 500 to the response hook.

## 0.0.11
- H19.11: the consumer projects of `tests/release-install` require the release 0.0.11: their manifests and locks name the archives `polyspec-hyper-client-npm-0.0.11.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-server-npm-0.0.11.tgz`, `polyspec-hyper-build-npm-0.0.11.tgz` and `polyspec-hyper-server-php-0.0.11.zip`, and `make release-consumer TAG=v0.0.11` installs and checks them (#H19.11).
- H19.9-3: HY-99 states how a PHP consumer receives the write result of a response. PHP has no write hook, because php-fpm sends the response; a consumer registers its own shutdown function, which knows its request from the start and reads `http_response_code()` there, as HY-67 does for the disconnect hook. Red: HY-99 named the Python and Node write channels only. Green: `make documents-check` passes 15 document pairs (#H19.9-3).
- H19.9-2: an error that escapes the answer of a request gets hyper's own plain 500 in every adapter, with the status 500, the text `Internal Server Error`, `Cache-Control: no-store` and the frame policy (HY-60). The WSGI and ASGI callables write that 500 before `start_response` or the first send instead of propagating the error (HY-100, HY-101). The write hook of the Python server receives it once with the request as received, also when `around` never ran (HY-99). PHP `App::run` writes it for an uncaught exception and for a fatal error of the request. Before, the Python callables propagated the error, `create_server` gave the write hook no report for it, and PHP let it reach the web server's 500. Red: `make test-python` ran 87 unit tests and failed with `failures=2, errors=8`, and `make test-php` ran 321 tests and failed with `Failures: 3`; the Node test passed at its first run, because the Node server already writes that 500 through its response. Green: `make test-python` passes 87 unit tests and 2 package tests, `make test-node` passes 338 tests and the type check, `make test-php` passes 321 tests, and `make test-python-render` passes 32 tests (#H19.9-2).
- H19.9-1: an error that escapes the answer of a request gets a plain 500 with the text `Internal Server Error` on the Node server and the Python `create_server`, as the production engines answer it, instead of a closed connection, which nginx answers with 502 Bad Gateway (HY-60). The server logs the message of HY-43 first, reports the 500 to no hook, and closes the connection when the response has started or the 500 cannot be written. Red: the Node response hook test received no 500 but a closed connection, the Python test raised `RemoteDisconnected`; Green: both servers answer the 500 with its frame policy and no cache, and no hook receives it (#H19.9-1).
- H19.9: the Node server writes no response for an error that escapes the answer of a request: it logs the error with the message of HY-43, closes the connection and calls no further response hook for that request, as the Python server does (HY-60). Before, the server wrote a plain 500 after such an error, and the response hook did not receive that response with its request. Red: a response hook that throws left the client a response with status 500 (`promise resolved "Response { status: 500`); Green: the client gets no response, the error is logged once and the hook is called once (#H19.9).
- H19.8: the dependency policy check reads the lock of `tests/package-install` as well as the root lock: `config/dependency-policy.json` names both in `npmLocks`, the kit tools are synced from v0.0.13, and the review record covers both locks. Red: a stale hyper-client version (0.0.7) in that lock fails the check with a finding; Green: the real lock passes with 0 findings (#H19.8).

## 0.0.10
- H19.4: the WSGI and ASGI callables read the request target that the server received, as `create_server` reads it: `create_wsgi` takes the target from `RAW_URI`, or else from `REQUEST_URI`, and `create_asgi` takes the path from `scope['raw_path']` decoded as latin-1. Before, both read the percent-decoded `PATH_INFO` and `scope['path']`, so `GET /notes/a%20b` reached the application as `/notes/a b` and `/a%2Fb` merged with `/a/b`. An encoded space, an encoded slash and a percent sequence now reach the application as sent (HY-100, HY-101). A WSGI environ without both keys and an ASGI scope without `raw_path` fail the request with an error that names the missing key.
- H19.4-1: the test `test_the_request_reaches_the_application` sends its body through the environ key `wsgi.input`; it passed the misspelled keyword `wsgi_input`, so the body was never read. The test now asserts that the application receives the value `hey` of the body (#H19.4-1). Red: with a broken body read the test before the fix passed, and the fixed test failed with `AssertionError: 'hey' != ''`. Green: `make test-python` passes its 80 unit tests and 2 package tests.
- H19.4-2: the test `test_a_body_over_the_limit_reads_no_more` sends its body through the environ key `wsgi.input` (it passed the misspelled keyword `wsgi_input`) and asserts that the reads total at most the body limit (HY-100, HY-59). The WSGI callable no longer reads a body whose Content-Length is over the limit; its size is its Content-Length. Red: the fixed test failed with `AssertionError: 25 not less than or equal to 16`, because the callable read the whole 25-byte body. Green: `make test-python` passes its 80 unit tests and 2 package tests.
- H19.7: the lock of the private test package `tests/package-install` records `@polyspec/hyper-client` and `@polyspec/hyper-server` at 0.0.10, the versions of the local packages, where it recorded 0.0.7 (#H19.7). The package keeps its own version 0.0.8, because it publishes nothing; AGENTS.md "Release" says the bump commit writes its lock again. Red: the lock recorded both packages at 0.0.7 (lines 20 and 29). Green: the lock records 0.0.10, and `make install` then `make package-check` passes its 4 tests.

## 0.0.9
- H19.3-1: the dependency policy of 0.0.9 passes. `package-lock.json` records `@polyspec/hyper-build`, `@polyspec/hyper-client` and `@polyspec/hyper-server` at 0.0.9, with their `@polyspec/hyper-client` requirement at 0.0.9; `@types/node` is 26.6.5, the latest stable release at the review; and the review record is written again for the three locks (#H19.3-1). Red: `node scripts/kit/check-dependency-policy.mjs` printed 6 findings. Green: it prints 0 findings and `make dependency-policy-mutation-check` passes.
- H19.2: the Python server package publishes an ASGI application callable: `create_asgi` of `polyspec.hyper.asgi` takes the arguments of `create_server` and returns an ASGI 3 callable that an ASGI server runs; it answers every request of the scope type `http` as `create_server` does, runs the pipeline of a request in a worker thread and reports a failed send to `written` before the disconnect hook (HY-101).
- H19.1: the Python server package publishes a WSGI application callable: `create_wsgi` of `polyspec.hyper.wsgi` takes the arguments of `create_server` and returns a callable that a WSGI server runs; it answers every request as `create_server` does, and `written` reports a write that the premature close of the body iterable ends (HY-100). The request pipeline of the three transports lies in `polyspec.hyper.pipeline`.
- H18.9: the documents name the Node.js and the Python server as implementations of the one server protocol, not as servers with the rules of the PHP server; the deployment document names the server as the renderer, and HY-62 and HY-92 name the Python forms of the selection, the data embedding and the client-rendering declaration.
- H18.8: README states the three servers: the introduction names the PHP, Node.js and Python server, the SSR and CSR modes and the router sentence name the server, and the conformance row of json.json names the Python server (HY-54).

The copy of the template repository (`scripts/copy-template.mjs`, the `template` target and the `var/products/template` copy) is removed, and `make template-tag` stays for the full-run key. The original acceptance (PIE from a Composer repository) could not be met before 0.1, because the project publishes to no Packagist. `tests/scripts/template-ext.test.mjs` and `tests/scripts/template-pins.test.mjs` replace `tests/scripts/template-copy.test.mjs`. - H17.1-1: `make release-proof` runs with `npm_config_allow_remote=root` like `make release-consumer`; without it the `npm ci` of the consumer project failed with `EALLOWREMOTE` for the template tarballs (npm 12.2.0). `release-consumer-lock` keeps `all` (H13.5-14). `tests/scripts/check-recipes.test.mjs` requires the value of each of the three targets. Verified: `make release-proof TAG=v0.0.5` passes.
- H15.3-20: the Python server calls the disconnect hook of HY-67. `App.open` takes `on_disconnect`, which fails when it is not callable; `App.respond` returns the response with the reply of the request, and `App.handle` returns the response. `create_server` writes the response after the response hook, and when a write fails with `BrokenPipeError`, `ConnectionResetError` or `ConnectionAbortedError` it calls the hook once with the request, the elapsed milliseconds and the reply; it does not detect a close when every write succeeds. HY-67 states the Python server. Verified: the 3 cases of `packages/hyper-python/tests/render/render_server.py` failed with `TypeError` for `on_disconnect` before the change and pass after it.
- H15.3-21: the Python server takes the request hook `around` of HY-97. `create_server` calls it with the request and `answer(request)`, which runs the application with the session of the request and the cookie of a new session, and sends the `Response` that it returns; `around` may change the request with the new `Request.with_header(name, value)`, change the response or answer alone, and then no session is opened and no hook of the application runs. The disconnect hook receives the request and the reply of the last `answer`. A value that is not callable fails when the server is created. HY-97 states the rule with the `request` listener of the Node server as reference. Verified: the 6 cases of `AroundTest` in `packages/hyper-python/tests/render/render_server.py` failed with `TypeError` for `around` and the case of `with_header` in `tests/test_modules.py` with `AttributeError` before the change, and all pass after it.

### Added

- The execution checklist opens the task H15.3 with its sub-items H15.3-1 to H15.3-5, and the wave 15 background names the Python implementation (H15.3).
- The Python package `packages/hyper-python` (`polyspec-hyper`, namespace `polyspec.hyper`) implements the router (HY-4 to HY-8, HY-49), the query and form values (HY-56, HY-57), the masked CSRF token (HY-24) and the JSON text with the bytes of the PHP server (HY-17, HY-54); its `unittest` tests read every case of `conformance/routes.json`, `rest.json`, `csrf.json`, `fields.json` and `json.json` (H15.3-1).
- The Python package adds the request with its query, form and selection values, the reply with its cookies, cache control, page status and data embedding, the results and stops of actions, the manifest with its regions and kept paths, the region selection of a response, the data model check, the kept values that conform (HY-37, HY-38) and the read paths that the data keeps (HY-73); the `unittest` tests read every case of `conformance/keep.json` (31) and `conformance/reads.json` (23) (H15.3-2).
- The Python package answers requests: `App.open` creates an application from its manifest, its handlers and the server program, `App.handle` answers one request with its document, JSON, action redirect or stop result (HY-8, HY-15 to HY-19, HY-24 to HY-27, HY-40, HY-50 to HY-62, HY-65 to HY-67), `ClientRendering` with `Choice` declares the client-rendered pages (HY-62) and the renderer renders with the template Python package; `make test-python-render` runs the rendering tests with the template package of the sibling checkout, and its `unittest` tests read every case of `conformance/client.json` (24) (H15.3-3).
- The Python package serves an application over the standard library `http.server`: `create_server` sends every request to `App.handle`, reads no more of a body larger than the body limit (HY-59), sets the cookie of a new session first (HY-45) and serves the files of an optional public directory, and `FileSessions` keeps each session in a JSON file named by an identifier that it created, written with one rename, with the requests of one session one after another; the board example server `examples/board/python` answers the parity requests of the board, and `make server-parity-python` compares its 65 responses with the PHP responses in status, headers and body, leaving the `Server` header of `http.server` out as the comparison leaves `X-Powered-By` of the PHP built-in server out (HY-55, H15.3-4).
- The CI group `python` of the Makefile (`CI_TARGETS_python`) runs `make test-python` in the matrix of `.github/workflows/ci.yml` on Python 3.11 and on the Python pin of `config/toolchain.json`, which `make ci-pins` gives as a step output beside the PHP minor; the group installs nothing, because the tests use only the standard library. The owner rules own the new paths, the feature status and the README tables name the Python server (H15.3-5).
- The execution checklist adds the rows H15.3-15 to H15.3-18: the `py.typed` package data of `packages/hyper-python`, the Python git dependency record, the feature status wording for the bypassed row H15.3-14, and the waiting release row of the package (H15.3).

### Fixed

- The Python tests that read a manifest or a program from a temporary directory remove that directory after the read (H15.3-6).
- The docstring and the test name of the region request check name the `HX-Request` header, not a client library (H15.3-7).
- The Python README names the kept functions by their module path, `polyspec.hyper.kept.apply` and `polyspec.hyper.kept.select` (H15.3-8).
- The Python README install line takes the release tag as the placeholder `vX.Y.Z`, because no release tag carries `packages/hyper-python` yet (H15.3-9).
- The `setuptools==80.9.0` pin of the Python package is recorded in the dependency record with its reason and its removal condition (H15.3-10).
- The H15.3 verification names `make test-python-render` and `make server-parity-python` as running outside CI until a template release carries the Python package (H15.3-11).
- The Python package's build backend pin changes from `setuptools==80.9.0` to `setuptools==84.0.0`, the release that the sibling Python packages pin (H15.3-13).
- The render and parity targets run in the CI entry `python-render` on the template release v0.0.5, which carries `packages/template-python` (H15.3-14).
- The package data of `packages/hyper-python` declares the `py.typed` marker of the namespace package `polyspec.hyper`, and `make test-python` runs the package data test of `tests/package` beside the unit tests (H15.3-15).
- The release declarations name the Python manifest `packages/hyper-python/pyproject.toml` in `NOT_RELEASED`, with its reason, so the release test accepts every tracked manifest (H15.3-19).

## 0.0.4

### Added

- The npm package `@polyspec/hyper-build` releases the build commands (H13.5-13, HY-96). Its bins `hyper-build-server` and `hyper-build-assets` replace `scripts/build-server.mjs` and `scripts/build-assets.mjs`, which consumers ran from a checkout and no release asset held; the package requires `@polyspec/hyper`, `@polyspec/template`, `@polyspec/template-compiler`, esbuild and Tailwind CSS at exact versions, and `make release-assets` writes `polyspec-hyper-build-X.Y.Z.tgz`. The builds read the manifest check, the read paths and the new export `@polyspec/hyper/data-template.json` of the published browser package instead of the source of `packages/hyper-js`, so `make server`, `make server-fixtures` and `make node-fixtures` now run `make packages` first. The Makefile runs the bins by their files, and the scripts of the repository import the modules of the package.

### Verification

- `tests/scripts/release-install.test.mjs` installs `polyspec-hyper-build-X.Y.Z.tgz` in the npm consumer fixture and builds the sample application `tests/release-install/app` with both bins of `node_modules/.bin`, with `--tailwind` (H13.5-13). npm blocks the `postinstall` script of esbuild in the consumer, and esbuild runs from its platform package. npm 12.2.0 refuses the bundled-dependency tarball of `@tailwindcss/oxide-wasm32-wasi` under `allow-remote=root` while it writes a lock (https://github.com/npm/cli/pull/9818), so `make release-fixtures` writes the fixture lock with `--allow-remote=all` until the pinned npm contains the fix (H13.5-14); `npm ci` of the fixture keeps `--allow-remote=root`.

## 0.0.3

### Changed

- npm and Composer install the template packages from the assets of the template release v0.0.4 (H14.1-5, HY-70, HY-78, HY-80). The private root `package.json` requires `@polyspec/template` and `@polyspec/template-compiler` by the URLs of their release tarballs with an override of `@polyspec/template`, `.npmrc` sets `allow-remote=root`, and `package-lock.json` pins each tarball by its URL and integrity; the private root `composer.json` and `examples/board/composer.json` take `polyspec/template` from a `package` repository of the release zip with its sha1 `shasum`. The published manifests require template 0.0.4 exactly. `scripts/build-server.mjs`, `scripts/build-assets.mjs` and `scripts/build-templates.mjs` import `@polyspec/template` and `@polyspec/template-compiler/*` by package name and no longer take `--template-dir`. The declared copy `var/products/template` holds only the native extension sources, its stub and its build script of the tag `TEMPLATE_TAG` (`v0.0.4`), which `make ext` and PHPStan read; `make template` installs nothing, and `make template-check` is removed. CI checks out the template tag without building it, and the consumer fixtures of `tests/release-install` take the template tarball and zip by their release URLs.

### Fixed

- The release assets are installed from committed consumer fixtures with their locks (H13.5-12, HY-89, HY-95). `tests/scripts/release-install.test.mjs` ran `npm install` without a lock, so npm resolved htmx.org from the registry when the test ran. `tests/release-install/npm` holds a `package.json` that depends on the release tarballs by `file:` and its `package-lock.json`, which pins htmx.org by its version and integrity; `tests/release-install/composer` holds a `composer.json` with an `artifact` repository of the release zips and its `composer.lock`. The test installs them in a temporary directory outside the repository with `npm ci` into an empty cache with the scope `@polyspec` pointed at an unreachable registry, and with `composer install` with an empty `COMPOSER_HOME` and cache. `make release-fixtures` writes the locks. HY-89 states that no check makes a registry query whose result depends on time and that a download pinned by a lock with its exact version and integrity is installation. `make lint` and `make analyse-php` start Pint and PHPStan from the root vendor directory `PHP_VENDOR`, and `toolchain.test.mjs` accepts the root `composer install`; both tests failed on the root vendor of H13.5-11.
- The release assets hold the manifests of the tree unchanged (H13.5-11, HY-95, HY-79). `make release-assets` rewrote the packed manifests, so a published package differed from its manifest in the tree. `packages/hyper-js/package.json` and `packages/hyper-node/package.json` require `@polyspec/template` 0.0.2 and `@polyspec/hyper` 0.0.3, and the private root `package.json` supplies the copies of the checkout through `file:` dependencies and `overrides`. `packages/hyper-php/composer.json` declares `version` 0.0.3, requires `polyspec/template` 0.0.2 and has no `repositories`; the private root `composer.json` and `composer.lock` install it for development into the root `vendor` from `var/products/hyper-php` and the template copy, and `examples/board/composer.json` requires `polyspec/template` 0.0.2. `make release-assets` packs the manifests unchanged and fails for a packed manifest that differs from its source manifest, and `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` checks the manifests of the tree. The npm install of `tests/scripts/release-install.test.mjs` takes htmx.org from the public registry into an empty cache of the test and points the scope `@polyspec` at an unreachable registry; with an empty npm cache the earlier `npm install --offline` failed with `ENOTCACHED` for htmx.org.
- The release assets install outside the repository (H13.5-9, HY-95). The packed manifests named `@polyspec/template` by a `file:` path into the checkout and `polyspec/template` by `@dev` with a path repository, and the zip had no `version`, so no consumer could install them. `make release-assets` packs each npm package, rewrites `package.json` of the packed copy and packs it again, and writes the zip with a rewritten `composer.json`: every polyspec dependency is the exact version of its release, a package of this repository at the version of the tag and a template package at the version of `TEMPLATE_TAG`, and the zip has `version` and no `repositories`. It fails and names the archive, the field and the value for a path, git, `github:`, ssh, URL, range or `@dev` dependency on a polyspec package, for `repositories` and for a missing version. The manifests of the repository keep their links into the checkout. `docs/operations/development.md` states how a consumer installs the assets: npm tarballs as `file:` dependencies, Composer zips from an `artifact` repository. Red: the check names 5 problems in the assets of v0.0.2. Green: `tests/scripts/release.test.mjs` passes 25 cases, and `tests/scripts/release-install.test.mjs` installs the packed assets with `npm install --offline` and with Composer from an `artifact` repository in a temporary directory outside the repository.

## 0.0.2

### Fixed
